## Combo 500-1777-2719-3191
Cards: Kairi, the Swirling Sky + Burnt Offering + Saw in Half + Sakashima of a Thousand Faces

Produces: Return all instant and sorcery cards from your graveyard to your hand; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite black mana; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite death triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite red mana; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite self-mill; Infinite storm count; Put all instant and sorcery cards from your graveyard and library into your hand
Start: Kairi, the Swirling Sky (battlefield); Burnt Offering (hand); Saw in Half (hand); Sakashima of a Thousand Faces (battlefield: as a copy of Kairi)
Steps: Cast Saw in Half by paying {2}{B}, destroying Sakashima of a Thousand Faces and creating two token copies of it that are nonlegendary copies of Kairi.
  When Sakashima of a Thousand Faces dies, it triggers.
  Holding priority, cast Burnt Offering by paying {B} and sacrificing one of the Sakashima of a Thousand Faces tokens.
  When the token dies, it triggers. Choose either mode.
  Resolve Burnt Offering, adding {B}{B}{B/R}{B/R}{B/R}{B/R}
  Resolve the Sakashima of a Thousand Faces trigger from step 5, causing you to mill six cards and return Saw in Half and Burnt Offering from your graveyard to your hand.
  Repeat
Notes: You can forego casting Burnt Offering if you have enough black mana. This'll allow you to net creature tokens. The additional Kairi trigger in step 4 can be used to put all instants and sorceries from your library and graveyard into your hand, or to bounce permanents.

  When the creature dies, Sidisi triggers, untapping Sidisi.
  Resolve the Sidisi ability, returning Bladewing from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  When Bladewing enters the battlefield, it triggers, returning the Dragon with mana value 6 from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Activate Grimgrin by sacrificing Bladewing.
  When Bladewing dies, Sidisi triggers, untapping Sidisi.
  Resolve the Grimgrin ability.
  Repeat.

  Activate Havengul Lich by paying {1}, allowing you to cast Impulsive Pilferer from your graveyard this turn.
  Cast Impulsive Pilferer from your graveyard by paying its casting cost.
  Repeat.

## Combo 745-3439-4613-7196
Cards: Grub, Storied Matriarch // Grub, Notorious Auntie + Mirrorhall Mimic // Ghastly Mimicry + Agatha's Soul Cauldron + Civilized Scholar // Homicidal Brute
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {3}{U}
Produces: Infinite card draw; Infinite draw triggers; Infinite self-discard triggers
Start: Grub, Storied Matriarch // Grub, Notorious Auntie (battlefield); Mirrorhall Mimic // Ghastly Mimicry (hand); Agatha's Soul Cauldron (battlefield); Civilized Scholar // Homicidal Brute (graveyard)
Steps: Cast Mirrorhall Mimic by paying {3}{U}. Choose to have it enter as a copy of Grub, Storied Matriarch.
  Put Grub, Storied Matriach into your graveyard due to the legend rule.
  When Mirrorhall Mimic enters, it triggers, returning Grub, Storied Matriarch from your graveyard to your hand.
  Activate Agatha's Soul Cauldron by tapping it and exiling Civilized Scholar from your graveyard, putting a +1/+1 counter on Mirrorhall Mimic.
  Activate Mirrorhall Mimic using Civilized Scholar's ability by tapping it, causing you to draw a card and discard Grub, Storied Matriarch. Mirrorhall Mimic will then untap itself and transform, but it will transform again into Grub, Storied Matriarch.
  Mirrorhall Mimic triggers, returning Grub, Storied Matriarch from your graveyard to your hand.
  Repeat from step 5.
Notes: This combo functions as Mirrorhall Mimic will only copy the characteristics from Grub, Storied Matriarch and not Grub, Notorious Auntie. This is outlined here: "712.8d While a double-faced permanent has its front face up, it has only the characteristics of its front face."

## Combo 771-1031-2276-3098-6229
Cards: Tetsuo, Imperial Champion + Savage Beating + Invasion of Arcavios // Invocation of the Founders + Said // Done + Call to Mind
Color identity: UBR | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite storm count; Infinite combat phases; Infinite untap of creatures you control; Infinite mana creatures you control can produce; Infinite magecraft triggers
Start: Tetsuo, Imperial Champion (battlefield); Savage Beating (hand); Invasion of Arcavios // Invocation of the Founders (battlefield); Said // Done (hand, graveyard); Call to Mind (hand, graveyard)
Prerequisites: Tetsuo has an Equipment attached to it with mana value 5 or greater.
  Tetsuo does not have summoning sickness and cannot be blocked and killed by an opponent.
  One of Call to Mind or Said must be in your hand, with the other in your graveyard.
Steps: Declare Tetsuo as an attacker. Tetsuo triggers, causing you to cast Savage Beating without paying its mana cost, choosing its second mode.
  Invocation of the Founders triggers, copying Savage Beating.
  Resolve both Savage Beatings, causing you to get two additional combat phases after this one.
  Move to your first combat phase.
  Declare Tetsuo as an attacker. Tetsuo triggers, causing you to cast Call to Mind without paying its mana cost, targeting Said.
  Invocation of the Founders triggers, copying Call to Mind, targeting Savage Beating.
  Resolve both Call to Minds, causing you to return Said and Savage Beating from your graveyard to your hand.
  Move to your second combat phase.
  Repeat steps 1 through 4 one additional time.
  Declare Tetsuo as an attacker. Tetsuo triggers, causing you to cast Said without paying its mana cost, targeting Call to Mind.
  Invocation of the Founders triggers, copying Said, targeting Savage Beating.
  Resolve both Saids, causing you to return Call to Mind and Savage Beating from your graveyard to your hand.
  Move to your second combat phase, and repeat from step 1.

Start: Tetsuo, Imperial Champion (battlefield); Savage Beating (hand); Swarm Intelligence (battlefield); Relearn (hand, graveyard); Sorceress's Schemes (hand, graveyard)

  Declare Tetsuo as an attacker. Tetsuo triggers, causing you to cast Relearn without paying its mana cost, targeting Sorceress's Schemes.

  Move to your second combat phase.

  Declare Tetsuo as an attacker. Tetsuo triggers, causing you to cast Sorceress's Schemes without paying its mana cost, targeting Relearn.
  Swarm Intelligence triggers, copying Sorceress's Schemes, targeting Savage Beating.
  Resolve both Sorceress's Schemess, causing you to return Relearn and Savage Beating from your graveyard to your hand.
  Move to your second combat phase, and repeat from step 1.
